entry-content container-lg" itemprop="text"><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Candidates</h1><a id="user-content-candidates" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Candidates" href="#candidates"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path <p dir="auto">Tracking which Lean packages we are considering for inclusion in
<code>lean-pool</code>, along with the reasoning behind each decision.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Layout</h2><a id="user-content-layout" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Layout" href="#layout"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>manual.txt</code> (committed) — hand-edited list of GitHub URLs for
packages that Reservoir does not index (forks, missing
<code>lake-manifest.json</code>, non-OSI license, fewer than two stars). One
URL per line; <code>#</code> comments and blank lines allowed.</li>
<li><code>CRITERIA.txt</code> (committed) — the inclusion policy used when
classifying a candidate as in / out of <code>lean-pool</code>.</li>
<li><code>decisions.jsonl</code> (committed) — one classifier verdict per line
(<code>repo</code>, <code>include</code>, <code>category</code>, <code>confidence</code>, <code>rationale</code>). Source
of truth for what shows up in the table below.</li>
<li><code>raw_data/</code> (gitignored) — local-only data fetched by
<code>python -m lean_pool.aggregator fetch</code>: the Reservoir manifest plus
GitHub metadata for each entry in <code>manual.txt</code>.</li>
<li>The summary table below is regenerated from those files by
<code>python -m lean_pool.aggregator render</code>. By default it shows only
packages that pass three filters: ≥ 2 stars (Reservoir's own floor),
≥ 250 lines of <code>.lean</code> code in the local clone, and <code>include=true</code>
in <code>decisions.jsonl</code> (repos with no verdict are kept regardless).
The LOC column counts every <code>.lean</code> file under the clone outside
<code>.lake</code>/<code>.git</code>/<code>build</code> directories. The In Pool column shows ✓ for
candidates already imported into lean-pool — it's joined from the
<code>source.github_repo</code> field of each project in <code>LeanPool/projects.yml</code>.
Pass <code>--min-stars=0 --min-loc=0 --decisions /dev/null</code> to disable all
three filters and render the full list.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Do not edit the autogenerated section by hand.</p>
